  Rollbar.configure({enabled: false});
}

function checkForAds() {
  var bait = document.getElementById("blocker-bait");

  if (bait == null) {
    disableRollbar();
    return;
  }

  var baitStyles = window.getComputedStyle(bait);
  if (baitStyles && (
      baitStyles.getPropertyValue('display') === 'none' ||
      baitStyles.getPropertyValue('visibility') === 'hidden')) {
    disableRollbar();
  }
}

function onLoadStartAdCheck() {
  // Ad blockers generally execute just after load, let's delay ourselves to get behind it.
  setTimeout(checkForAds, 1);
}

if (window.addEventListener !== undefined) {
  window.addEventListener('load', onLoadStartAdCheck, false);
} else {
  window.attachEvent('onload', onLoadStartAdCheck);
}</script><!--[if lte IE 9]><div class="fixed w-100 h-100 f3 fw3 bg-cerulean z-9999 fg-white overflow-hidden"><div class="dn dib-ns fg-white" id="nere_logo_large"><svg role="image" class="w17 h12 ma9" alt="NejŘemeslníci logo"><use xlink:href="https://www.nejremeslnici.cz/assets/svg/icons-6a2f1268bf05aa938aa523793bc3a91f66eb7524efbe2bed2042984d514e9fb0.svg#icons-logo"><title></title></use></svg></div><p class="mt12 mh-auto tc lh-copy">Používáte <span class="fw7">zastaralou verzi prohlížeče</span> Internet Explorer. aria-autocomplete="list" autocomplete="off" class="string optional w-100 pa6 bg-white b--solid bw1 b-white-40 br2 shadow-inset1 input-reset" id="q" maxlength="50" name="q" size="50" type="text"/><ul .2s}.alt_placeholder{transition:opacity .2s;pointer-events:none}input[type="text"]:focus+.alt_placeholder{opacity:0}.awesomplete .visually-hidden{display:none}.awesomplete li[aria-selected="true"]{background-color:#009EE0}.awesomplete ul mark{background-color:transparent;font-weight:bold}.awesomplete ul .nz-pseudo::before{display:block;position:absolute;left:0;padding-left:1rem;padding-right:.5rem}.awesomplete ul .nz-empty{margin-top:4.5rem}.awesomplete ul .nz-empty::before{padding-top:1rem;padding-bottom:2rem;top:-5rem;font-style:italic;color:gray;font-size:90%;width:100%}</style><script>var phrase_on_load = ""
var currentList = []
$(document).ready(function()
{
  //TODO: deal with the magical value of 984
  var awesomplete = new Awesomplete(document.querySelector("#q"), {
    minChars: 1,
    maxItems: $(window).width() > 984 ? 11 : 4,
    filter: function () {
      return true;
    },
    item: function (item, query) {
      $item = $("<li>");

      if(item.value.pseudo == "empty") {
        $item.addClass("nz-empty nz-pseudo relative h0 pa0");
        $("body").append("<style>.nz-empty::before { content: 'Nic nenalezeno' } </style>");
      }
      else if(item.value.tag) {
        $item.html(item.value.name_marked);
      }
      return $item[0];
    },
    replace: function (item) {
      if (item.value.tag) this.input.value = item.value.tag.tag.name;
      if (item.value.url) window.location = item.value.url;
    },
    sort: function() { return false }
  });

  $("#q").on("awesomplete-select", function(e) {
    if($(e.originalEvent.origin).hasClass("nz-pseudo")) {
      e.preventDefault();
    }
  });

  var currItemIndex;

  $("#q").on("awesomplete-open", function() {
    currItemIndex = -1;
  });

  /*
    The following listener is a hack that achieves that navigation using arrow keys still works
    in the autocompleter even when pseudo items are present. Note that the same thing could not be managed using
    Awesomplete's 'highlight' event as it doesn't pass any information revealing identity of the highlighted item.
  */
  $(".awesomplete").on("keyup", function(e) {
    if(e.which == 38) {
      if(--currItemIndex < 0) currItemIndex = currentList.length - 1;
    }
    else if(e.which == 40) {
      if(++currItemIndex == currentList.length) currItemIndex = 0;
    }
  });

  //Handles switching size of the autocompleter list
  $(window).on("resize", function()
  {
    awesomplete.maxItems = $(window).width() > 984 ? 11 : 4;
  });

  var prevPhrase = null;
  var itemsByPhrase = {};

  function autocomplete(e, skip)
  {
    var code = e.keyCode || e.which;
    var phrase = $(this).val().trim();
    if(code === 13/*enter key*/ || phrase.length === 0 || skip) return;

    setTimeout((function(phrase)
    {
      if(phrase !== $("#q").val().trim()) return;

      var list = itemsByPhrase[phrase];
      if(typeof list === "undefined")
      {
        itemsByPhrase[phrase] = true;
        $.get("/tags-autocomplete", "q=" + phrase, function(response)
        {
          phrase = response.phrase.trim();
          var list = response.list.slice();
          itemsByPhrase[phrase] = list;
          if(phrase === $("#q").val().trim() && phrase !== prevPhrase)
          {
            currentList = awesomplete.list = list;
            prevPhrase = phrase;
          }
        });
      }
      else if(phrase !== prevPhrase && typeof list == "object")
      {
        currentList = awesomplete.list = list;
        prevPhrase = phrase;
      }
    }).bind(this, phrase), 300);
  }

  $("#q").on("keyup", autocomplete);
  $("#q").on("focus", autocomplete);

  //handles the situation when user manages to fill the input before awesomplete is initialized
  setTimeout(function() { if($("#q").val() !== phrase_on_load) $("#q").trigger("focus"); }, 0);

  $("#q").on("focus", function()
  {
    var items = itemsByPhrase[$("#q").val()];
    if(typeof items === "object" && items.length > 0)
    {
      currentList = awesomplete.list = items;
      awesomplete.open();
    }
  });

  $(".nz-search-tag-form input[type='text']").on("focus", function(){
    var parent = $(this).closest(".input");
    parent.children("label").css({opacity: 1});
    parent.children(".alt_placeholder").css("opacity", "0");
    parent.removeClass("has_error");
  }).on("blur", function(){
    var parent = $(this).closest(".input");
    parent.children("label").css({opacity: 0});
    parent.children(".alt_placeholder").css("opacity", "1");
    input_placeholder($(this));
  });

  function input_placeholder($this) {
    var placeholder = $this.closest(".input").children(".alt_placeholder");
    placeholder.css("display", ($this.val() !== "") ? "none" : "inline");
  }

  // Hack for IE10 and below
  $(".input").on("click", function()
  {
    $(this).find("input").trigger("focus");
  });

  var input = $("#q")[0];
  var length = input.value.length;
  if (length > 0) {
    $(input).trigger("focus", true);
    input.setSelectionRange(length, length);
  }
});</script><a class="dib mt8 mt0-l f6 fg-white ts2 ts0-l no-underline border-underline-thin v-mid" href="/page/user_intro">Jak to funguje?</a></div></div></div></div></section><section class="section-transition-arrow pt10 pb13 tc fw3 bg-outer-space z-1 overflow-hidden" id="section-themes"><picture class="absolute w-100 h-100 top-0 left-0 z--1"><source media="(max-width: 511px)" srcset="https://i.nejz.cz/assets/backgrounds/www-theme-bg_512-a2d40a40541f76e09158f8095578948763796200d1a3904c969ec17074e670b4.jpg 1x,
                           https://i.nejz.cz/assets/backgrounds/www-theme-bg_512@2x-bcac95f8ca4df16e778a4ee762ed33f8261314c0c385585528555012f04e00a4.jpg 2x"/><source media="(max-width: 1023px)" srcset="https://i.nejz.cz/assets/backgrounds/www-theme-bg_1024-bcac95f8ca4df16e778a4ee762ed33f8261314c0c385585528555012f04e00a4.jpg 1x,

  var $themeBox = $(".theme-box"),
      $subthemes = $(".nz-subthemes"),
      $nz_show_more = $(".nz-show-more");

  $themeBox.on("mouseenter mouseleave", function () {
    var $this = $(this);
    $themeBox.removeClass("hover").addClass("disable");
    $nz_show_more.addClass("o-50");
    $this.addClass("hover");
    $subthemes.removeClass("show");
    $("#" + $this.data("main")).addClass("show");
  }, function () {

  });
  $(".nz-theme-section").on("mouseenter mouseleave", function () {

  }, function () {
    $themeBox.removeClass("hover");
    $subthemes.removeClass("show");
    $themeBox.removeClass("disable");
    $nz_show_more.removeClass("o-50");
  });
  $subthemes.on("mouseenter mouseleave", function () {
    $subthemes.removeClass("show");
    $(this).addClass("show");
  }, function () {
  });

  $(".nz-show-more").on("click", function () {
    var $target = $("#" + $(this).data("target"));
    $(this).addClass("dn");
    $target.removeClass("dn");
    $('html,body').animate({scrollTop: $target.offset().top - 90}, 500);
  });
